# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# MODELI S VISOKIM RIZIKOM — morfo_validator se uvijek pokreće
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
HIGH_RISK_MODELI = {
    "llama31_70b_cerebras",
    "llama33_70b_groq",
    "llama_sambanova",
    "qwen_chutes",
}

# Threshold qualiteta ispod kojeg se pokušava retry s drugim modelom
QUALITY_RETRY_THRESHOLD = 6.5
MAX_RETRIES = 3


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# ANTI-PATTERN ČIŠĆENJE
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _ocisti_anti_patterne(tekst: str, model_ime: str) -> str:
    """
    Uklanja per-model anti-patterne iz izlaza (meta-komentari, markdown blokovi).
    Poziva se PRIJE quality scoringa.
    """
    if not _profiles_ok:
        return tekst

    patterne = get_anti_patterne(model_ime)
    ocisceno = tekst

    for pattern in patterne:
        try:
            ocisceno = re.sub(pattern, "", ocisceno, flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE)
        except re.error as e:
            logger.warning(f"Anti-pattern regex greška ({pattern}): {e}")

    # Ukloni prazne linije na početku/kraju nastale čišćenjem
    ocisceno = ocisceno.strip()
    return ocisceno


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# WORKER KLASA
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class WorkerV2:
    """
    Worker za obradu jednog chunka s V10.4 pipeline-om.
    Backward compatible — može se koristiti kao zamjena za stari Worker.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        uloga: str = "prevodilac",
        tip_bloka: Optional[str] = None,
        book_context: Optional[Any] = None,
        chapter_summary: Optional[str] = None,
        few_shot_primjeri: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    ):
        """
        Args:
            uloga: "prevodilac" | "lektor" | "validator"
            tip_bloka: "dijalog" | "poetski" | "naracija" | "tehnicki" | None
            book_context: BookContextManager instanca (za glosar i vlastita imena)
            chapter_summary: summary tekućeg poglavlja (iz book_context.py)
            few_shot_primjeri: 3-5 primjera dobrog prijevoda iz iste knjige
        """
        self.uloga = uloga
        self.tip_bloka = tip_bloka
        self.book_context = book_context
        self.chapter_summary = chapter_summary
        self.few_shot_primjeri = few_shot_primjeri or []

    def _sklopi_prompt(self, model_ime: str, chunk_tekst: str) -> str:
        """Sklapa kompletan system prompt za ovaj chunk i model."""
        if _injector_ok:
            try:
                injector = PromptInjector()
                return injector.assemble(
                    uloga=self.uloga,
                    model=model_ime,
                    tip_bloka=self.tip_bloka,
                    glosar=getattr(self.book_context, "glosar", {}),
                    chunk_text=chunk_tekst,
                    chapter_summary=self.chapter_summary,
                    few_shot_primjeri=self.few_shot_primjeri,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"PromptInjector greška: {e} — koristim fallback prompt")

        # Fallback: prompts_v2 bez injectora
        if _prompts_v2_ok:
            try:
                return get_system_prompt(
                    uloga=self.uloga,
                    model_ime=model_ime,
                    tip_bloka=self.tip_bloka,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"prompts_v2 greška: {e} — koristim minimal prompt")

        # Minimal fallback
        return (
            "Ti si književni prevodilac. Pišeš hrvatski, ijekavica. "
            "Vraćaš samo prevedeni tekst, bez komentara."
        )

    def _api_poziv(
        self,
        provider: str,
        model_string: str,
        api_key: Optional[str],
        system_prompt: str,
        chunk_tekst: str,
        model_ime: str,
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Obavlja API poziv prema provideru.
        Vraća odgovor kao string ili None pri grešci.
        Integrira se s postojećim http_client/network slojem.
        """
        try:
            from network.http_client import api_call
        except ImportError:
            logger.error("network.http_client nije dostupan")
            return None

        temperatura = get_temp(model_ime, self.uloga) if _profiles_ok else 0.75
        max_tok = get_max_tokens(model_ime, self.uloga) if _profiles_ok else 2048

        try:
            odgovor = api_call(
                provider=provider,
                model=model_string,
                api_key=api_key,
                system=system_prompt,
                user=chunk_tekst,
                temperature=temperatura,
                max_tokens=max_tok,
            )
            return odgovor
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"API poziv greška ({provider}/{model_string}): {e}")
            return None

    def _postprocessing(self, tekst: str, model_ime: str) -> str:
        """
        Post-processing pipeline:
        1. Anti-pattern čišćenje (per-model)
        2. KalkoviEngine deterministička korekcija
        3. MorfoValidator (za high-risk modele ili uvijek za validator ulogu)
        """
        # 1. Anti-pattern čišćenje
        tekst = _ocisti_anti_patterne(tekst, model_ime)

        # 2. KalkoviEngine — primijeni() vraća (tekst, n_zamjena) tuple
        if _kalkovi_ok:
            glosar = getattr(self.book_context, "glosar", {}) if self.book_context else {}
            try:
                tekst, _ = kalkovi_engine.primijeni(tekst, glosar=glosar)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"KalkoviEngine greška: {e} — originalni tekst prolazi")

        # 3. MorfoValidator
        pokreni_validator = (
            model_ime in HIGH_RISK_MODELI
            or self.uloga == "validator"
        )
        if pokreni_validator and _validator_ok:
            try:
                tekst = morfo_validator.validiraj(tekst)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"MorfoValidator greška: {e} — tekst prolazi bez validacije")

        # 4. Rod detektor — aktivna korekcija miješanja roda
        if _ROD_DETEKTOR_AKTIVAN and _rod_detektor is not None:
            try:
                knjiga_id = (
                    getattr(self.book_context, "knjiga_id", "")
                    or getattr(self.book_context, "naziv", "")
                    if self.book_context else ""
                )
                glosar_rod = {}
                if self.book_context:
                    raw_glosar = getattr(self.book_context, "_glosar", {})
                    glosar_rod = {
                        ime: entry.get("rod")
                        for ime, entry in raw_glosar.items()
                        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("rod") in ("M", "Ž")
                    }
                tekst, n_rod = _rod_detektor.primijeni(
                    tekst, knjiga_id=knjiga_id, glosar_rod=glosar_rod
                )
                if n_rod:
                    logger.info(f"[rod_detektor] {n_rod} rod korekcija primijenjena")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"RodDetektor greška: {e} — tekst prolazi nepromijenjen")

        return tekst

    def obradi_chunk(
        self,
        chunk_tekst: str,
        exclude_modeli: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Glavna metoda. Obrađuje jedan chunk kroz kompletan V10.4 pipeline.

        Returns:
            {
                "tekst": str,           # obrađeni tekst
                "model_ime": str,       # korišteni model
                "provider": str,        # korišteni provider
                "quality_score": float, # quality score (0–10)
                "retries": int,         # broj ponovnih pokušaja
                "success": bool,        # da li je obrada uspjela
            }
        """
        exclude = list(exclude_modeli or [])
        retries = 0
        zadnji_tekst = chunk_tekst  # fallback = original

        while retries < MAX_RETRIES:
            # 1. Odabir modela
            if _router_v2_ok:
                routing = provider_router_v2.get_best_model(
                    uloga=self.uloga,
                    tip_bloka=self.tip_bloka,
                    exclude=exclude,
                )
            else:
                routing = None

            if not routing:
                logger.error("Nema dostupnih modela — vraćam originalni tekst")
                return {
                    "tekst": zadnji_tekst,
                    "model_ime": "none",
                    "provider": "none",
                    "quality_score": 0.0,
                    "retries": retries,
                    "success": False,
                }

            provider, model_string, api_key = routing

            # Pronađi model_ime po api_model_string-u (za profil lookup)
            model_ime = next(
                (ime for ime, p in PROFILI.items() if p.api_model_string == model_string),
                model_string,
            ) if _profiles_ok else model_string

            # 2. Sklopi prompt
            system_prompt = self._sklopi_prompt(model_ime, chunk_tekst)

            # 3. API poziv
            odgovor = self._api_poziv(
                provider=provider,
                model_string=model_string,
                api_key=api_key,
                system_prompt=system_prompt,
                chunk_tekst=chunk_tekst,
                model_ime=model_ime,
            )

            if not odgovor:
                logger.warning(f"Prazan odgovor od {model_ime} — pokušavam drugi model")
                exclude.append(model_ime)
                retries += 1
                continue

            # 4. Post-processing
            obradjeni = self._postprocessing(odgovor, model_ime)
            zadnji_tekst = obradjeni

            # 5. Quality scoring
            quality_score = 0.0
            if _quality_ok:
                try:
                    quality_score = ocijeni_kvalitet(obradjeni, chunk_tekst)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"Quality scoring greška: {e}")
                    quality_score = 7.0  # default

            logger.info(
                f"WorkerV2: chunk obrađen — model={model_ime} "
                f"quality={quality_score:.1f} retries={retries}"
            )

            # Korak 11a — Pasivni detektor (ne utjece na prijevod)
            if _DETEKTOR_AKTIVAN and _detektor:
                try:
                    _knjiga = (
                        getattr(self.book_context, "file_name", "")
                        or getattr(self.book_context, "naziv", "")
                        if self.book_context else ""
                    )
                    _detektor.analiziraj(
                        original=chunk_tekst,
                        prijevod=obradjeni,
                        knjiga=_knjiga,
                        chunk_idx=0,
                        quality_score=quality_score,
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass  # Detektor nikad ne smije prekinuti pipeline

            # 6. Retry ako je quality prenizak
            if quality_score < QUALITY_RETRY_THRESHOLD and retries < MAX_RETRIES - 1:
                logger.warning(
                    f"Quality {quality_score:.1f} < {QUALITY_RETRY_THRESHOLD} — "
                    f"pokušavam bolji model (retry {retries + 1})"
                )
                exclude.append(model_ime)
                retries += 1
                continue

            return {
                "tekst": obradjeni,
                "model_ime": model_ime,
                "provider": provider,
                "quality_score": quality_score,
                "retries": retries,
                "success": True,
            }

        # Iscrpljeni retry-ji — vraćamo zadnji tekst
        logger.error(f"Iscrpljeni retry-ji ({MAX_RETRIES}) — vraćam zadnji obrađeni tekst")
        return {
            "tekst": zadnji_tekst,
            "model_ime": "fallback",
            "provider": "fallback",
            "quality_score": 0.0,
            "retries": retries,
            "success": False,
        }


    def pokreni_qa_benchmark(
        self,
        file_name: str,
        quality_scores_path: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Korak 10: QA Benchmark — bez AI-a.
        Poziva se jednom po knjizi nakon što su svi chunkovi obrađeni.
        Sprema logs/qa_baseline_<knjiga>_<datum>.json i trend fajl.
        """
        if not _qa_benchmark_ok:
            return
        try:
            _qa_benchmark.analiziraj_fajl(
                file_name=file_name,
                quality_scores_path=quality_scores_path,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"qa_benchmark greška za {file_name}: {e}")


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# CONVENIENCE FUNKCIJA — drop-in zamjena za stari workers.py
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def obradi_chunk_v2(
    chunk_tekst: str,
    uloga: str = "prevodilac",
    tip_bloka: Optional[str] = None,
    book_context: Optional[Any] = None,
    chapter_summary: Optional[str] = None,
    few_shot_primjeri: Optional[List[str]] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Drop-in zamjena za stari obradi_chunk() poziv.
    Koristi kompletan V10.4 pipeline.
    """
    worker = WorkerV2(
        uloga=uloga,
        tip_bloka=tip_bloka,
        book_context=book_context,
        chapter_summary=chapter_summary,
        few_shot_primjeri=few_shot_primjeri,
    )
    return worker.obradi_chunk(chunk_tekst)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("WorkerV2 učitan. HIGH_RISK_MODELI:", HIGH_RISK_MODELI)
    print("KalkoviEngine dostupan:", _kalkovi_ok)
    print("MorfoValidator dostupan:", _validator_ok)
    print("RouterV2 dostupan:", _router_v2_ok)
    print("PromptsV2 dostupan:", _prompts_v2_ok)

"""epub/styler.py — BOOKLYFI STYLER v2 (konačna verzija)
===========================================================================
JEDINSTVENI FORMAT ZA SVE KNJIGE.

PRAVILA:
  1. Obriši SVE staro (css, klase, dropcap, ornament, sadržaj, toc, ncx)
  2. Normalizuj HTML (div→p, h4-h6→h3, font→unwrap, deduplikacija AA→A)
  3. Detektuj STVARNA poglavlja (isključi uvod/o autoru/impresum/sadržaj)
  4. Za svako poglavlje: vijenac → POGLAVLJE N → dropcap
  5. Dropcap: 4em, pokriva 3+ reda, prvo slovo nevidljivo ali u tekstu
  6. Novi toc.ncx i toc.xhtml — SAMO stvarna poglavlja
  7. Svi TOC-ovi i ncx-ovi iz EPUB-a se brišu, pravi se jedan
===========================================================================
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import copy
import logging
import os
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from bs4 import BeautifulSoup, NavigableString

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

STYLED_OUTPUT_DIR = "/storage/emulated/0/Books/MoonReader/STYLED"
ORNAMENTS_DIR = "/storage/emulated/0/Books/MoonReader/ornaments_small_size"
CENTER_ORNAMENT_PNG = "center_ornament.png"


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 1. PNG SISTEM
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

_PNG_CACHE: dict[str, str] = {}


def _png_b64(fname: str) -> str:
    if fname in _PNG_CACHE:
        return _PNG_CACHE[fname]
    path = os.path.join(ORNAMENTS_DIR, fname)
    if os.path.exists(path):
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            b64 = base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode("utf-8")
            _PNG_CACHE[fname] = b64
            return b64
    return ""


def _scan_dropcaps() -> dict[str, str]:
    m = {}
    if not os.path.isdir(ORNAMENTS_DIR):
        return m
    for fname in sorted(os.listdir(ORNAMENTS_DIR)):
        if not fname.lower().endswith(".png"):
            continue
        stem = fname[:-4]
        if len(stem) in (1, 2) and stem.isalpha():
            m.setdefault(stem.upper(), fname)
    return m


DROPCAP_MAP = _scan_dropcaps()


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 2. CSS — JEDAN STIL
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

CSS = """
body { background:#FDF6E3; color:#3E2723; font-family:Georgia,serif;
       font-size:1em; line-height:1.6; margin:0 5%; padding:0; }
p { margin:0; padding:0; text-indent:1.8em; font-size:1em; line-height:1.6; }
p.booklyfi-prvi { text-indent:0; margin-top:0.5em; margin-bottom:0.3em; }
p.booklyfi-dijalog { margin:0.3em 0; padding-left:1.5em; text-indent:-1em; }
h1.booklyfi-naslov { color:#5D4037; text-align:center;
    font-size:1.8em; font-weight:600; letter-spacing:0.05em;
    margin:0.5em 0 0.8em 0; line-height:1.3; text-indent:0; }
img.booklyfi-vijenac { display:block; width:70%; height:auto;
    margin:0.5em auto 0.8em auto; }
img.booklyfi-dropcap { float:left; display:inline;
    width:4em; height:auto; max-width:none;
    margin:0.1em 0.15em 0 0; padding:0; vertical-align:top; }
em, i { font-style:italic; }
strong, b { font-weight:700; }
"""


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 3. BRISANJE SVE STARE STILIZACIJE
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _ukloni_sve(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> None:
    # Style/link
    for style in soup.find_all("style"):
        style.decompose()
    for link in soup.find_all("link"):
        rel = link.get("rel") or []
        if isinstance(rel, str):
            rel = [rel]
        if "stylesheet" in [r.lower() for r in rel]:
            link.decompose()

    # Ornamenti
    for tag in soup.find_all("img", class_="booklyfi-vijenac"):
        tag.decompose()
    for tag in soup.find_all("img", class_="booklyfi-ornament"):
        tag.decompose()
    for tag in soup.find_all("div", class_="booklyfi-ornament"):
        tag.decompose()
    for tag in soup.find_all("p", class_="booklyfi-vijenac"):
        tag.decompose()

    # Dropcapi
    for tag in soup.find_all("img", class_="booklyfi-dropcap"):
        tag.decompose()
    for tag in soup.find_all("span", class_="booklyfi-dropcap"):
        tag.decompose()

    # Skrivena slova — VRATI SLOVO U TEKST
    for span in soup.find_all("span", style=True):
        s = span.get("style") or ""
        if "font-size:1px" in s or "font-size: 1px" in s or "font-size:0" in s:
            slovo = span.get_text(strip=True)
            span.replace_with(slovo)

    # Klase booklyfi-*
    for el in soup.find_all(class_=True):
        classes = el.get("class") or []
        if isinstance(classes, str):
            classes = classes.split()
        new_c = [c for c in classes if not c.startswith("booklyfi-")]
        if new_c:
            el["class"] = new_c
        elif el.get("class") is not None:
            del el["class"]


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 4. NORMALIZACIJA HTML-a
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _normalizuj(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> None:
    # div → p
    for div in soup.find_all("div"):
        if div.find(["div", "p", "ul", "ol", "table"]):
            continue
        if not div.get_text(strip=True):
            continue
        div.name = "p"

    # h4-h6 → h3
    for h in soup.find_all(["h4", "h5", "h6"]):
        h.name = "h3"

    # font → unwrap
    for font in soup.find_all("font"):
        font.unwrap()

    # center → p
    for c in soup.find_all("center"):
        p_new = soup.new_tag("p")
        for child in list(c.children):
            p_new.append(child)
        c.replace_with(p_new)

    # table layout → p
    for table in soup.find_all("table"):
        if len(table.find_all("tr")) <= 1:
            p_new = soup.new_tag("p")
            p_new.string = table.get_text(" ", strip=True)
            table.replace_with(p_new)

    # span bez stila → unwrap
    for span in soup.find_all("span"):
        if not span.get("style"):
            span.unwrap()

    # b→strong, i→em
    for b in soup.find_all("b"):
        b.name = "strong"
    for i in soup.find_all("i"):
        i.name = "em"

    # u,s,strike → unwrap
    for tag in soup.find_all(["u", "s", "strike", "del", "ins"]):
        tag.unwrap()

    # Deduplikacija: AAutorska → Autorska
    for text_node in soup.find_all(string=True):
        if text_node.parent and text_node.parent.name in ("script", "style"):
            continue
        orig = str(text_node)
        new = re.sub(r'\b([A-ZŠĐČĆŽ])\1+([a-zšđčćž])', r'\1\2', orig)
        if new != orig:
            text_node.replace_with(new)


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 5. DIJALOG
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _dijalog(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> int:
    try:
        from core.kalkovi.dijalog import DIJALOG_TIPOGRAFIJA
    except Exception:
        return 0
    n = 0
    for node in soup.find_all(string=True):
        if node.parent and node.parent.name in ("script", "style"):
            continue
        orig = str(node)
        new = orig
        for pattern, repl in DIJALOG_TIPOGRAFIJA:
            new = re.sub(pattern, repl, new)
        if new != orig:
            node.replace_with(new)
            n += 1
    return n


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 6. VIJENAC
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _dodaj_vijenac(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> None:
    b64 = _png_b64(CENTER_ORNAMENT_PNG)
    body = soup.find("body")
    if not body:
        return
    if b64:
        html = (f'<img class="booklyfi-vijenac" '
                f'src="data:image/png;base64,{b64}" '
                f'style="display:block;width:70%;height:auto;'
                f'margin:0.5em auto 0.8em auto;" />')
    else:
        html = '<p class="booklyfi-vijenac" style="text-align:center;">— ✠ —</p>'
    orn = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser").find(["img", "p"])
    body.insert(0, copy.deepcopy(orn))


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 7. NASLOV
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _dodaj_naslov(soup: BeautifulSoup, broj: int) -> None:
    body = soup.find("body")
    if not body:
        return
    # Obriši sve h1-h3 (stari naslovi)
    for h in soup.find_all(["h1", "h2", "h3"]):
        h.decompose()
    h1 = soup.new_tag("h1")
    h1["class"] = "booklyfi-naslov"
    h1.string = f"POGLAVLJE {broj}"
    # Ubaci poslije vijenca (ako postoji), inače na početak
    insert_pos = 0
    for i, child in enumerate(body.children):
        classes = child.get("class") if hasattr(child, "get") else None
        if classes:
            if isinstance(classes, str):
                classes = classes.split()
            if "booklyfi-vijenac" in classes:
                insert_pos = i + 1
                continue
        break
    body.insert(insert_pos, h1)


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 8. DROPCAP — 4em, 3+ reda, prvo slovo nevidljivo u tekstu
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _dodaj_dropcap(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> bool:
    for p in soup.find_all("p"):
        # Preskoči naslove
        classes = p.get("class") or []
        if isinstance(classes, str):
            classes = classes.split()
        if "booklyfi-naslov" in classes:
            continue

        text = p.get_text(strip=True)
        if len(text) < 60:
            continue
        if text.isdigit():
            continue
        if text[0] in ("—", "–", "-", "‒", "―"):
            continue
        if text[0] in ('"', '„', '“', '«', "'"):
            continue

        # Prvo slovo
        first_char = None
        for content in p.contents:
            if isinstance(content, NavigableString):
                s = str(content).strip()
                if s:
                    first_char = s[0].upper()
                break
            elif hasattr(content, "get_text"):
                t = content.get_text(strip=True)
                if t:
                    first_char = t[0].upper()
                break

        if not first_char or not first_char.isalpha():
            continue

        fname = DROPCAP_MAP.get(first_char)
        if not fname:
            continue
        b64 = _png_b64(fname)
        if not b64:
            continue

        img_html = (
            f'<img class="booklyfi-dropcap" '
            f'src="data:image/png;base64,{b64}" '
            f'style="float:left;display:inline;'
            f'width:4em;height:auto;max-width:none;'
            f'margin:0.1em 0.15em 0 0;padding:0;'
            f'vertical-align:top;" />'
        )

        novi_html = f'<p class="booklyfi-prvi">{img_html}'
        novi_html += f'<span style="font-size:1px;color:transparent;line-height:0;">{first_char}</span>'

        skip = True
        for content in list(p.contents):
            if isinstance(content, NavigableString):
                s = str(content)
                if skip:
                    stripped = s.lstrip()
                    lead = len(s) - len(stripped)
                    s = s[:lead] + stripped[1:]
                    skip = False
                novi_html += s
            else:
                novi_html += str(content)

        novi_html += "</p>"
        novi_p = BeautifulSoup(novi_html, "html.parser").find("p")
        if novi_p:
            p.replace_with(novi_p)
            return True

    return False


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 9. DETEKCIJA STVARNIH POGLAVLJA
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

SKIP_FNAME = (
    "cover", "titlepage", "title_page", "copyright",
    "colophon", "toc", "contents", "nav",
    "impressum", "about", "acknowledg", "dedication",
    "epigraph", "foreword", "preface", "introduction",
)

SKIP_TITLES = (
    "sadržaj", "sadrzaj", "table of contents", "contents", "toc",
    "impresum", "copyright", "autorska prava", "sva prava",
    "naslovna", "title page", "cover", "korice",
    "uvod", "uvodne informacije", "uvodna", "predgovor",
    "prolog", "foreword", "preface", "introduction",
    "epilog", "pogovor", "afterword", "epilogue",
    "o autoru", "o piscu", "o knjizi", "o izdanju", "about",
    "bilješka", "biljeska", "napomena", "note",
    "posveta", "dedication", "zahvala", "zahvale",
    "izjave zahvalnosti", "acknowledgments",
    "kazalo", "dodatak", "appendix", "index", "glossary",
    "prolaznost života", "prolaznost zivota",
)

SKIP_FIRST_P = (
    "naslov izvornika", "autorska prava", "copyright",
    "sadržaj", "uvod", "predgovor", "posveta",
    "zahvala", "bilješka", "napomena", "izvorni naslov",
    "izvorno izdanje", "originalno izdanje",
)


def _je_poglavlje(html_bytes: bytes, full_path: str) -> bool:
    # Veličina
    if len(html_bytes) < 8000:
        return False

    # Ime fajla
    fname_low = full_path.lower()
    if any(s in fname_low for s in SKIP_FNAME):
        return False

    # Sadržaj
    try:
        soup = BeautifulSoup(html_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), "html.parser")
    except Exception:
        return False

    text = soup.get_text(strip=True)
    if len(text) < 5000:
        return False

    # Naslov
    title = ""
    for tag in ["h1", "h2", "h3", "title"]:
        t = soup.find(tag)
        if t:
            txt = t.get_text(strip=True)
            if txt:
                title = txt
                break

    title_low = title.lower().strip()
    for skip in SKIP_TITLES:
        if title_low == skip or title_low.startswith(skip + " ") or title_low.startswith(skip + ":"):
            return False

    # Prvi <p>
    first_p = soup.find("p")
    if first_p:
        fp_low = first_p.get_text(strip=True).lower()[:100]
        for skip in SKIP_FIRST_P:
            if fp_low.startswith(skip):
                return False

    return True


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 10. EPUB I/O
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _read(path: Path) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as z:
        return {n: z.read(n) for n in z.namelist()}


def _write(path: Path, files: dict[str, bytes]) -> None:
    tmp_fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".epub.tmp", dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(tmp, "w") as z:
            if "mimetype" in files:
                z.writestr(zipfile.ZipInfo("mimetype"), files["mimetype"],
                           compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_STORED)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(tmp, "a", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
            for n, d in files.items():
                if n == "mimetype":
                    continue
                z.writestr(n, d)
        os.close(tmp_fd)
        shutil.move(tmp, str(path))
    except Exception:
        try:
            os.close(tmp_fd)
            os.unlink(tmp)
        except Exception:
            pass
        raise


def _meta(files: dict) -> tuple[str, str]:
    for n, d in files.items():
        if n.endswith(".opf"):
            try:
                s = BeautifulSoup(d, "xml")
                t = s.find("dc:title")
                c = s.find("dc:creator")
                return (
                    c.get_text(strip=True) if c else "Nepoznat_autor",
                    t.get_text(strip=True) if t else "Nepoznat_naslov",
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
    return "Nepoznat_autor", "Nepoznat_naslov"


def _san(s: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r'[\\/:*?"<>|]', "", s.strip())
    return re.sub(r"\s+", "_", s)


def _ime(c: str, t: str) -> str:
    return f"{_san(c)}_-_{_san(t)}_PREVEDENO_STYLED.epub"


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 11. GLAVNA
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def run_epub_styler(epub_path, output_path=None, fleet=None,
                    log_callback=None, options=None) -> dict:
    epub_path = Path(epub_path)

    def _log(msg, atype="info"):
        logger.info("[styler] %s", msg)
        if log_callback:
            log_callback(msg, atype)

    # ── BOOKLYFI STRANICE (nested — bez NameError) ──
    def _napravi_naslovnu(creator, title):
        import datetime
        year = datetime.datetime.now().year
        css = ("body{background:#FDF6E3;color:#3E2723;font-family:Georgia,serif;"
               "margin:0;padding:3em 5% 2em 5%;text-align:center;}"
               ".booklyfi-label{font-size:0.85em;letter-spacing:0.3em;"
               "color:#8B6914;text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:4em;}"
               ".title{font-size:2.4em;font-weight:700;color:#5D4037;"
               "letter-spacing:0.05em;line-height:1.2;margin:2em 0 0.5em 0;}"
               ".author{font-size:1.3em;color:#3E2723;letter-spacing:0.08em;"
               "margin-top:3em;}"
               ".publisher{font-size:0.9em;color:#8B6914;letter-spacing:0.15em;"
               "margin-top:5em;text-transform:uppercase;}"
               ".rule{border:none;border-top:1px solid #8B6914;width:40%;"
               "margin:2em auto;opacity:0.5;}")
        return ('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>'
                '<!DOCTYPE html>'
                '<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="hr">'
                f'<head><meta charset="utf-8"/><title>Naslovna</title>'
                f'<style>{css}</style></head>'
                '<body>'
                '<div class="booklyfi-label">Booklyfi izdanje</div>'
                '<hr class="rule"/>'
                f'<h1 class="title">{title}</h1>'
                '<hr class="rule"/>'
                f'<p class="author">{creator}</p>'
                f'<div class="publisher">Booklyfi &middot; {year}</div>'
                '</body></html>')

    def _napravi_impresum(creator, title, uid=""):
        import datetime
        year = datetime.datetime.now().year
        uid_line = f'<p><span class="label">ID:</span> {uid}</p>' if uid else ''
        css = ("body{background:#FDF6E3;color:#3E2723;font-family:Georgia,serif;"
               "margin:0 8%;padding:3em 0 2em 0;line-height:1.7;font-size:0.95em;}"
               "h1{color:#5D4037;text-align:center;font-size:1.3em;"
               "letter-spacing:0.15em;margin-bottom:2em;}"
               "h2{color:#5C3A1E;font-size:1em;letter-spacing:0.1em;"
               "margin-top:1.5em;margin-bottom:0.5em;font-style:italic;}"
               "p{margin:0.4em 0;text-indent:0;}"
               ".meta{margin-bottom:2em;}"
               ".meta .label{color:#8B6914;font-size:0.85em;"
               "letter-spacing:0.08em;text-transform:uppercase;}"
               ".booklyfi-block{margin-top:3em;padding-top:1.5em;"
               "border-top:1px solid #8B6914;color:#5C3A1E;font-size:0.9em;}"
               ".center{text-align:center;color:#8B6914;letter-spacing:0.2em;"
               "margin:2em 0;}")
        return ('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>'
                '<!DOCTYPE html>'
                '<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="hr">'
                f'<head><meta charset="utf-8"/><title>Impresum</title>'
                f'<style>{css}</style></head>'
                '<body>'
                '<h1>IMPRESUM</h1>'
                '<div class="meta">'
                f'<p><span class="label">Naslov:</span> {title}</p>'
                f'<p><span class="label">Autor:</span> {creator}</p>'
                f'<p><span class="label">Izdanje:</span> Booklyfi digitalno izdanje, {year}.</p>'
                f'{uid_line}'
                '</div>'
                '<div class="center">- - -</div>'
                '<h2>O ovom izdanju</h2>'
                '<p>Ovo izdanje pripremljeno je kroz <strong>Booklyfi</strong> '
                'pipeline za pripremu digitalnih izdanja knjizevnih djela. '
                'Tekst je prosao kroz automatsku lekturu, korekturu i '
                'tipografsku obradu.</p>'
                '<h2>Autorska prava</h2>'
                '<p>Sva prava zadrzana. Nijedan dio ove knjige ne smije se '
                'reproducirati, pohranjivati u sustavu za pretrazivanje ili '
                'prenositi u bilo kojem obliku bez prethodnog pisanog '
                'dopustenja nositelja prava.</p>'
                '<p>Digitalno izdanje pripremljeno za osobnu upotrebu.</p>'
                '<h2>Tehnicki podaci</h2>'
                '<p><span class="label">Format:</span> EPUB 3</p>'
                '<p><span class="label">Pismo:</span> Latinica (ijekavica)</p>'
                '<p><span class="label">Tipografija:</span> Georgia, EB Garamond</p>'
                '<div class="booklyfi-block">'
                '<p style="text-align:center;letter-spacing:0.15em;">BOOKLYFI</p>'
                f'<p style="text-align:center;font-size:0.85em;">Digitalna izdanja &middot; {year}</p>'
                '</div>'
                '</body></html>')

    result = {"ok": False, "epub_path": "", "chapters": 0,
              "dropcaps_added": 0, "toc_created": False, "error": None}

    if not epub_path.exists():
        result["error"] = f"Ne postoji: {epub_path}"
        return result

    _log(f"📖 {epub_path.name}", "system")

    try:
        files = _read(epub_path)
    except Exception as e:
        result["error"] = f"Čitanje: {e}"
        return result

    # Izlaz
    if output_path:
        out = Path(output_path)
    else:
        c, t = _meta(files)
        od = Path(STYLED_OUTPUT_DIR)
        od.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        out = od / _ime(c, t)

    # Nađi OPF
    opf_path = next((n for n in files if n.endswith(".opf")), None)
    if not opf_path:
        result["error"] = "Nema OPF"
        return result

    opf_soup = BeautifulSoup(files[opf_path], "xml")
    spine = opf_soup.find("spine")
    manifest = opf_soup.find("manifest")
    if not spine or not manifest:
        result["error"] = "Nema spine/manifest"
        return result

    id_to_href = {}
    for it in manifest.find_all("item"):
        iid = it.get("id", "")
        href = it.get("href", "")
        if href.lower().endswith((".html", ".xhtml", ".htm")):
            id_to_href[iid] = href

    opf_dir = str(Path(opf_path).parent)
    if opf_dir == ".":
        opf_dir = ""

    # Nađi poglavlja
    poglavlja = []
    for itemref in spine.find_all("itemref"):
        iid = itemref.get("idref", "")
        href = id_to_href.get(iid, "")
        if not href:
            continue
        full = f"{opf_dir}/{href}" if opf_dir else href
        hb = files.get(full) or files.get(href)
        if not hb:
            continue
        if _je_poglavlje(hb, full):
            poglavlja.append({
                "href": href, "full_path": full,
                "order": len(poglavlja) + 1,
            })

    _log(f"   → {len(poglavlja)} stvarnih poglavlja", "tech")

    # ── BOOKLYFI STRANICE (naslovna + impresum) ──
    creator_name, book_title = _meta(files)
    book_uid = "booklyfi"
    for n, d in files.items():
        if n.endswith(".opf"):
            try:
                s = BeautifulSoup(d, "xml")
                u = s.find("dc:identifier")
                if u:
                    book_uid = u.get_text(strip=True)
            except Exception:
                pass
            break

    naslovna_path = f"{opf_dir}/booklyfi_001_naslovna.xhtml" if opf_dir else "booklyfi_001_naslovna.xhtml"
    impresum_path = f"{opf_dir}/booklyfi_002_impresum.xhtml" if opf_dir else "booklyfi_002_impresum.xhtml"

    files[naslovna_path] = _napravi_naslovnu(creator_name, book_title).encode("utf-8")
    files[impresum_path] = _napravi_impresum(creator_name, book_title, book_uid).encode("utf-8")

    # Dodaj u OPF manifest
    opf_str_tmp = files[opf_path].decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    naslovna_href = Path(naslovna_path).name
    impresum_href = Path(impresum_path).name
    opf_str_tmp = opf_str_tmp.replace(
        "</manifest>",
        f'<item id="booklyfi-naslovna" href="{naslovna_href}" media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>'
        f'<item id="booklyfi-impresum" href="{impresum_href}" media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>'
        "</manifest>"
    )
    # Dodaj na početak spine-a
    opf_str_tmp = re.sub(
        r'(<spine[^>]*>)',
        r'\1<itemref idref="booklyfi-naslovna"/><itemref idref="booklyfi-impresum"/>',
        opf_str_tmp,
        count=1,
    )
    files[opf_path] = opf_str_tmp.encode("utf-8")
    _log(f"   → Booklyfi naslovna + impresum dodani", "tech")

    # Obrada poglavlja
    dropcaps = 0
    for p in poglavlja:
        full = p["full_path"]
        soup = BeautifulSoup(files[full].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), "html.parser")

        _ukloni_sve(soup)
        _normalizuj(soup)
        _dijalog(soup)
        _dodaj_vijenac(soup)
        _dodaj_naslov(soup, p["order"])
        if _dodaj_dropcap(soup):
            dropcaps += 1

        # CSS
        head = soup.find("head")
        if not head:
            head = soup.new_tag("head")
            if soup.html:
                soup.html.insert(0, head)
            else:
                soup.insert(0, head)
        st = soup.new_tag("style", type="text/css")
        st.string = CSS
        head.append(st)

        files[full] = str(soup).encode("utf-8")

    _log(f"   → {dropcaps} dropcapa", "tech")

    # ── NE BRISI HTML FAJLOVE - sve ostaje ──
    poglavlja_paths = {p["full_path"] for p in poglavlja}

    # ── Ne brisi stare TOC/NCX ──

    # Novi NCX
    book_title = "Knjiga"
    book_uid = "booklyfi"
    for n, d in files.items():
        if n.endswith(".opf"):
            s = BeautifulSoup(d, "xml")
            t = s.find("dc:title")
            if t:
                book_title = t.get_text(strip=True)
            u = s.find("dc:identifier")
            if u:
                book_uid = u.get_text(strip=True)
            break

    ncx_path = f"{opf_dir}/toc.ncx" if opf_dir else "toc.ncx"
    navpoints = "\n".join(
        f'  <navPoint id="np-{p["order"]}" playOrder="{p["order"]}">'
        f'<navLabel><text>POGLAVLJE {p["order"]}</text></navLabel>'
        f'<content src="{p["href"]}"/></navPoint>'
        for p in poglavlja
    )
    files[ncx_path] = f'''<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<ncx xmlns="http://www.daisy.org/z3986/2005/ncx/" version="2005-1">
<head><meta name="dtb:uid" content="{book_uid}"/><meta name="dtb:depth" content="1"/></head>
<docTitle><text>{book_title}</text></docTitle>
<navMap>
{navpoints}
</navMap>
</ncx>'''.encode("utf-8")

    # Novi TOC HTML
    toc_path = f"{opf_dir}/booklyfi_toc.xhtml" if opf_dir else "booklyfi_toc.xhtml"
    items = "\n".join(
        f'<li><a href="{p["href"]}">POGLAVLJE {p["order"]}</a></li>'
        for p in poglavlja
    )
    files[toc_path] = f'''<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="hr">
<head><meta charset="utf-8"/><title>Sadržaj</title>
<style>body{{background:#FDF6E3;color:#3E2723;font-family:Georgia,serif;margin:0 8%;padding:2em 0;}}
h1{{text-align:center;color:#5D4037;}}ul{{list-style:none;padding:0;}}
li{{padding:0.4em 0;border-bottom:1px dotted #8B6914;}}
a{{color:#3E2723;text-decoration:none;}}</style></head>
<body><h1>Sadržaj</h1><ul>
{items}
</ul></body></html>'''.encode("utf-8")

    # Ažuriraj OPF
    opf_str = files[opf_path].decode("utf-8", errors="replace")

    # Ukloni iz manifesta sve iteme koji referenciraju obrisane fajlove
    sve_u_epub = set(files.keys())
    def _ukloni_item(match):
        href = match.group(1)
        # Ako fajl ne postoji u files, ukloni item
        for n in files:
            if n.endswith(href) or href.endswith(Path(n).name):
                return match.group(0)  # zadrži
        return ''  # ukloni

    # Jednostavnije: prođi kroz manifest i ukloni iteme čiji href ne postoji
    manifest_soup = BeautifulSoup(opf_str, "xml")
    manifest_el = manifest_soup.find("manifest")
    if manifest_el:
        for item in manifest_el.find_all("item"):
            href = item.get("href", "")
            # Provjeri da li postoji u files
            if opf_dir:
                full_href = f"{opf_dir}/{href}"
            else:
                full_href = href
            if full_href not in files and href not in files:
                item.decompose()
        # Ukloni iz spine-a itemref koji referenciraju nepostojeće iteme
        spine_el = manifest_soup.find("spine")
        if spine_el:
            valid_ids = {it.get("id") for it in manifest_el.find_all("item")}
            for itemref in spine_el.find_all("itemref"):
                iid = itemref.get("idref")
                if iid and iid not in valid_ids and iid != "booklyfi-toc":
                    itemref.decompose()
        opf_str = str(manifest_soup)
    # Ukloni stare (ali NE booklyfi-naslovna/impresum)
    opf_str = re.sub(r'<item[^>]*id="(?:booklyfi-toc|ncx|toc)"[^>]*/>', '', opf_str, flags=re.I)
    opf_str = re.sub(r'<itemref[^>]*idref="(?:booklyfi-toc|toc)"[^>]*/>', '', opf_str, flags=re.I)
    # Dodaj nove
    opf_str = opf_str.replace("</manifest>",
        f'<item id="ncx" href="toc.ncx" media-type="application/x-dtbncx+xml"/>'
        f'<item id="booklyfi-toc" href="booklyfi_toc.xhtml" media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>'
        "</manifest>")
    opf_str = re.sub(r'(<spine[^>]*>)', r'\1<itemref idref="booklyfi-toc"/>', opf_str, count=1)
    if 'toc="ncx"' not in opf_str[:2000]:
        opf_str = re.sub(r'<spine(?![^>]*\btoc=)', '<spine toc="ncx"', opf_str, count=1)
    files[opf_path] = opf_str.encode("utf-8")

    # Snimi
    _log("💾 Snimam...", "tech")
    _write(out, files)

    result.update({
        "ok": True, "epub_path": str(out),
        "chapters": len(poglavlja),
        "dropcaps_added": dropcaps,
        "toc_created": True,
    })
    _log(f"✅ {out.name} ({len(poglavlja)} pogl, {dropcaps} dropcap)", "success")
    return result


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Upotreba: python -m epub.styler <knjiga.epub> [izlaz.epub]")
        sys.exit(1)
    res = run_epub_styler(
        sys.argv[1],
        output_path=sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else None,
        log_callback=lambda m, t="info": print(f"  {m}"),
    )
    print(f"\n{'✅ OK' if res['ok'] else '❌ ' + str(res['error'])}")
    if res["ok"]:
        print(f"   EPUB:      {res['epub_path']}")
        print(f"   Poglavlja: {res['chapters']}")
        print(f"   Dropcaps:  {res['dropcaps_added']}")


================================================================================
FAJL: epub/normalizer.py
VELICINA: 14062 bajtova

"""
epub/normalizer.py

Čisti markup koji je "preživio" iz ranijih obrada prije nego
run_epub_styler() doda svjež ornament/dropcap. Bez ovoga:
- stari, CSS-klasa-bazirani "span.dropcap" (definisan u
  _EPUB_PAPER_CSS, epub/styling.py) ostaje zalijepljen na
  pogrešnom paragrafu (često na samom naslovu poglavlja ako taj
  naslov nije bio ispravno označen kao <h1> u ranijem pokretanju
  parsera) i pravi vizuelni bug: "P" + "ROLOG" umjesto "PROLOG".
- ako se Stil tab pokrene DRUGI put na već stiliziranoj knjizi,
  novi booklyfi-dropcap/booklyfi-vijenac elementi bi se dodali
  NA VRH postojećih, umjesto da ih zamijene -> dupliranje.

normalize_chapter_html(soup) uklanja oba traga PRIJE nego
run_epub_styler pozove _add_chapter_ornament()/_add_dropcap().
"""

import re
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup, NavigableString


def normalize_chapter_html(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> None:
    """
    Mutira soup in-place. Ne vraća ništa (isti obrazac kao
    _add_chapter_ornament / _inject_epub_global_css).
    """
    _strip_legacy_dropcap_span(soup)
    _strip_booklyfi_dropcap(soup)
    _strip_booklyfi_vijenac(soup)


def _unwrap_preserving_text(tag, replacement_text: str) -> None:
    """Zamijeni tag čistim tekstom na istom mjestu u stablu."""
    tag.replace_with(NavigableString(replacement_text))


def _strip_legacy_dropcap_span(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> None:
    """
    Uklanja STARI <span class="dropcap">X</span> (jednina, bez
    "booklyfi-" prefiksa — iz span.dropcap CSS pravila u
    _EPUB_PAPER_CSS). Slovo se vraća kao običan tekst na početak
    paragrafa.
    """
    for span in soup.find_all("span", class_="dropcap"):
        letter = span.get_text()
        _unwrap_preserving_text(span, letter)


def _strip_booklyfi_dropcap(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> None:
    """
    Uklanja dropcap koji je NAŠA verzija _add_dropcap() već
    ubacila u ranijem pokretanju Stil taba (<img class=
    "booklyfi-dropcap"> ili <span class="booklyfi-dropcap">),
    tako da se ponovno pokretanje ne duplira i _add_dropcap()
    može ispravno prepoznati paragraf iznova.
    """
    for tag in soup.find_all(["img", "span"], class_="booklyfi-dropcap"):
        if tag.name == "img":
            letter = tag.get("alt", "")
        else:
            letter = tag.get_text()
        _unwrap_preserving_text(tag, letter)


def _strip_booklyfi_vijenac(soup: BeautifulSoup) -> None:
    """
    Uklanja centralni ornament (<img>/<p class="booklyfi-
    vijenac">) koji je _add_chapter_ornament() već ubacio u
    ranijem pokretanju — potpuno se briše (nije dio teksta, ne
    treba ga vraćati kao tekst), da svjež poziv doda tačno jedan.
    """
    for tag in soup.find_all(["img", "p"], class_="booklyfi-vijenac"):
        tag.decompose()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# POTPUNI STRIP — svaka knjiga izlazi identično bez obzira na ulaz
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════



def _normalize_html(soup):
    """
    Normalizuje HTML u jedinstvenu strukturu (Calibre-style).
    
    SVE knjige, bez obzira na izvor, izlaze sa:
      - <p> za tekst (ne <div>)
      - <h1>-<h3> za naslove (ne <h4>-<h6>)
      - <em>/<i>, <strong>/<b> za naglašavanje
      - <br> za prelom reda
      - Bez <font>, <center>, <table> layout
    
    Poziva se PRIJE _strip_everything.
    """
    from bs4 import NavigableString
    
    # ── 1. <div> sa tekstom → <p> ──
    for div in soup.find_all("div"):
        # Preskoči ako je strukturni wrapper (sadrži <div>/<p>/<ul>/<ol>)
        if div.find(["div", "p", "ul", "ol", "table"]):
            continue
        # Preskoči ako je prazan
        if not div.get_text(strip=True):
            continue
        # Prebaci u <p>
        div.name = "p"
        # Ukloni klase (Calibre klase)
        if div.get("class"):
            del div["class"]
    
    # ── 2. <h4>-<h6> → <h3> ──
    for h in soup.find_all(["h4", "h5", "h6"]):
        h.name = "h3"
    
    # ── 3. <font> → tekst sa <em>/<strong> ako treba ──
    for font in soup.find_all("font"):
        # Ako ima color/size, pretvori u <span style>
        style_parts = []
        if font.get("color"):
            style_parts.append(f"color:{font['color']}")
        if font.get("face"):
            style_parts.append(f"font-family:{font['face']}")
        
        if style_parts:
            span = soup.new_tag("span")
            span["style"] = ";".join(style_parts)
            font.replace_with(span)
            for child in list(font.children):
                span.append(child)
        else:
            font.unwrap()  # <font> bez stila → samo tekst
    
    # ── 4. <center> → <p style="text-align:center"> ──
    for c in soup.find_all("center"):
        p = soup.new_tag("p")
        p["style"] = "text-align:center;text-indent:0;"
        for child in list(c.children):
            p.append(child)
        c.replace_with(p)
    
    # ── 5. <table> layout → <p> ──
    for table in soup.find_all("table"):
        rows = table.find_all("tr")
        cells = table.find_all(["td", "th"])
        # Ako nije prava tabela (samo layout)
        if len(rows) <= 1 or len(cells) <= 1:
            p = soup.new_tag("p")
            p.string = table.get_text(" ", strip=True)
            table.replace_with(p)
    
    # ── 6. <span> bez stila/klase → unwrap ──
    for span in soup.find_all("span"):
        if not span.get("style") and not span.get("class") and not span.get("id"):
            span.unwrap()
    
    # ── 7. <b> → <strong>, <i> → <em> (semantički) ──
    for b in soup.find_all("b"):
        b.name = "strong"
    for i in soup.find_all("i"):
        i.name = "em"
    
    # ── 8. Deduplikacija duplih slova na početku riječi (AAutorska → Autorska) ──
    import re as _re
    for text_node in soup.find_all(string=True):
        if text_node.parent and text_node.parent.name in ("script", "style"):
            continue
        original = str(text_node)
        # Nađi riječi koje počinju sa 2+ istih slova
        new_text = _re.sub(
            r'\b([A-ZŠĐČĆŽ])\1+([a-zšđčćž])',
            r'\1\2',
            original
        )
        if new_text != original:
            text_node.replace_with(new_text)
    
    # ── 8. <u>, <s>, <strike> → ukloni (bez stila) ──
    for u in soup.find_all(["u", "s", "strike", "del", "ins"]):
        u.unwrap()
    
    return soup




def _ocisti_json_artefakte(html: str) -> str:
    """Cisti JSON artefakte iz AI odgovora."""
    # Markdown code fence
    html = re.sub(r'```json\s*', '', html)
    html = re.sub(r'```\s*', '', html)
    # JSON omotaci
    html = re.sub(
        r'\{\s*"(?:tekst|text|translation|content|polished|result|html)"\s*:\s*"',
        '', html
    )
    html = re.sub(r'"\s*\}\s*$', '', html, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    # Escape sekvence
    html = html.replace('\\n', '\n')
    html = html.replace('\\"', '"')
    html = html.replace('\\/', '/')
    # Samostalne viticaste zagrade
    html = re.sub(r'^\s*[\{\}]\s*$', '', html, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    return html

def _strip_everything(soup: BeautifulSoup, sacuvaj_tekst: bool = True) -> dict:
    """
    Uklanja SVE stilove, klase i nepotrebne elemente iz HTML-a — ALI:
      - Zadržava <h1>-<h6> (naslovi poglavlja — AI ili originalni)
      - Zadržava TOC elemente (<nav epub:type="toc">, <a href>, <ol>, <li>)
      - Zadržava <section> (strukturni wrapper)
      - Vraća skrivena slova u tekst (bez gubitka slova)

    Cilj: goli HTML koji se može ponovo stilizovati bez dupliranja.
    Onda stil_ai.py primjenjuje svoj jedinstveni stil.
    """
    stats = {
        "style_uklonjen": 0,
        "link_uklonjen": 0,
        "klasa_uklonjena": 0,
        "inline_style_uklonjen": 0,
        "img_uklonjen": 0,
        "span_uklonjen": 0,
        "div_uklonjen": 0,
        "skriveno_slovo_vraceno": 0,
        "toc_zadrzan": 0,
    }

    # ─── 1. Ukloni SVE <style> (i naše i tuđe) ────────────────
    for style in soup.find_all("style"):
        style.decompose()
        stats["style_uklonjen"] += 1

    # ─── 2. Ukloni SVE <link rel="stylesheet"> ────────────────
    for link in soup.find_all("link"):
        rel = link.get("rel") or []
        if isinstance(rel, str):
            rel = rel.split()
        if "stylesheet" in [r.lower() for r in rel]:
            link.decompose()
            stats["link_uklonjen"] += 1

    # ─── 3. Vrati skrivena slova iz svih <span style="font-size:1px;..."> ─
    for span in soup.find_all("span", style=True):
        s = span.get("style") or ""
        if "font-size:1px" in s or "font-size: 1px" in s or "visibility:hidden" in s:
            t = span.get_text()
            if t:
                span.replace_with(NavigableString(t))
                stats["skriveno_slovo_vraceno"] += 1
            else:
                span.decompose()

    # ─── 4. Vrati tekst iz <span class="booklyfi-dropcap"> ────
    for span in soup.find_all("span", class_="booklyfi-dropcap"):
        t = span.get_text()
        if t:
            span.replace_with(NavigableString(t))
        else:
            span.decompose()

    # ─── 5. Ukloni SVE <img> (dropcap/ornament/slike) ─────────
    for img in soup.find_all("img"):
        alt = (img.get("alt") or "").strip()
        # Vrati slovo u tekst samo ako je jedan znak (dropcap)
        if alt and len(alt) <= 2:
            img.replace_with(NavigableString(alt))
            stats["skriveno_slovo_vraceno"] += 1
        else:
            img.decompose()
        stats["img_uklonjen"] += 1

    # ─── 6. Ukloni SVE <span> — tekst vrati u parent ──────────
    for span in soup.find_all("span"):
        t = span.get_text()
        if t:
            span.replace_with(NavigableString(t))
        else:
            span.decompose()
        stats["span_uklonjen"] += 1

    # ─── 7. Ukloni SVE <div> osim ako je TOC ili section wrapper ─
    for div in soup.find_all("div"):
        klase = div.get("class") or []
        if isinstance(klase, str):
            klase = klase.split()
        # ZADRŽI ako je unutar TOC-a
        parent_nav = div.find_parent("nav")
        if parent_nav and "toc" in str(parent_nav.get("epub:type", "")).lower():
            continue
        # Ako je div prazan (nema teksta unutra) — obriši
        if not div.get_text(strip=True):
            div.decompose()
            stats["div_uklonjen"] += 1
            continue
        # Ako sadrži samo blok elemente — unwrap
        sadrzi_samo_blok = all(
            (not hasattr(c, "name")) or c.name in ("p", "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6", "br", "section", "blockquote", "ol", "ul", "li", "a", "nav")
            for c in div.children
        )
        if sadrzi_samo_blok:
            div.unwrap()
            stats["div_uklonjen"] += 1

    # ─── 8. Ukloni klase sa tagova — ALI ZADRŽI TOC tagove ────
    for tag in soup.find_all(class_=True):
        # Provjeri da li je TOC element
        parent_nav = tag.find_parent("nav")
        is_toc = parent_nav and "toc" in str(parent_nav.get("epub:type", "")).lower()
        # Provjeri da li je <a> unutar TOC-a
        if tag.name == "a" and parent_nav:
            is_toc = True
        if is_toc:
            stats["toc_zadrzan"] += 1
            continue
        klase = tag.get("class") or []
        if isinstance(klase, str):
            klase = klase.split()
        if klase:
            stats["klasa_uklonjena"] += len(klase)
            del tag["class"]

    # ─── 9. Ukloni inline stilove — ALI ZADRŽI TOC ────────────
    for tag in soup.find_all(style=True):
        parent_nav = tag.find_parent("nav")
        if parent_nav and "toc" in str(parent_nav.get("epub:type", "")).lower():
            continue
        del tag["style"]
        stats["inline_style_uklonjen"] += 1

    # ─── 10. Ukloni <body> stilove i klase ────────────────────
    body = soup.find("body")
    if body:
        for attr in ("style", "class", "bgcolor", "background", "text", "link"):
            if body.get(attr):
                del body[attr]


    # ─── 11. Obrisi prazne <p>*</p> i <p> </p> ───────────────────
    for p in soup.find_all("p"):
        tekst = p.get_text(strip=True)
        if tekst in ("*", "", "\u00a0") or (len(tekst) <= 2 and tekst.strip("* ") == ""):
            p.decompose()
            stats.setdefault("prazne_p_obrisane", 0)
            stats["prazne_p_obrisane"] += 1

    # ─── 12. Obrisi <div> sa inline background (AI artefakti) ────
    for div in soup.find_all("div"):
        stil = (div.get("style") or "").lower()
        if "background" in stil:
            # Ako je <div> omotac (sadrzi <p>, <h1>...), unwrap
            if div.find(["p", "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4"]):
                div.unwrap()
            else:
                div.decompose()
            stats.setdefault("ai_div_obrisan", 0)
            stats["ai_div_obrisan"] += 1

    # ─── 13. Obrisi <span> unutar <p class="booklyfi-prvi"> ─────
    for p in soup.find_all("p", class_="booklyfi-prvi"):
        for span in p.find_all("span"):
            t = span.get_text()
            if t:
                span.replace_with(NavigableString(t))
            else:
                span.decompose()
            stats.setdefault("span_u_prvi_obrisan", 0)
            stats["span_u_prvi_obrisan"] += 1

    return stats

FAJL: config/ai_config.py
VELICINA: 29736 bajtova

# config/ai_config.py

"""Central AI configuration shared across routing, quota, and model layers."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List

# RPD limiti (free tier, maj 2026):
#   gemini-3.5-flash:      10 RPM / 20 RPD  — primarni, stabilan
#   gemini-3.1-flash-lite: 15 RPM / 500 RPD — visok RPD fallback
#   gemini-2.5-flash-lite: 15 RPM / 20 RPD
#   gemini-2.5-flash:      10 RPM / 20 RPD
GOOGLE_MODEL_POOL = [
    # Primarni: gemini-3.5-flash-lite — najnoviji free-tier model
    # Test 2026-10-10: daje najbolji književni prijevod (aorist, bolji red riječi)
    # 15 RPM / 500 RPD po ključu × 12 ključeva = 180 RPM / 6000 RPD
    {"model": "gemini-3.5-flash-lite", "rpm": 15, "rpd": 500},
    # Fallback: gemini-3.1-flash-lite — stabilan, isti limiti
    {"model": "gemini-3.1-flash-lite", "rpm": 15, "rpd": 500},
]

GEMMA_MODEL_POOL = [
    {"model": "gemma-4-26b-a4b-it", "rpm": 15, "rpd": 1500},
    {"model": "gemma-4-31b-it", "rpm": 15, "rpd": 1500},
]

# GEMMA je nositelj (#1) za sve Google uloge — 1500 RPD/ključ.
# GEMINI je fallback (#2).
PROVIDER_PRIORITY = {
    # LEKTOR/KOREKTOR/PREVODILAC: GEMINI #1 (15 RPM, dovoljno za 3 poziva/blok)
    # GROQ #2 samo kao fallback (OTPM 1000 je tijesan)
    "PREVODILAC":       ["GEMINI", "GROQ", "NVIDIA", "GEMMA", "OPENROUTER", "CLOUDFLARE", "MISTRAL"],
    "LEKTOR":           ["GEMINI", "GROQ", "NVIDIA", "GEMMA", "OPENROUTER", "CLOUDFLARE", "MISTRAL"],

MORFO_VALIDATOR_MODEL = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite"

GEMINI_BASE_URL = "https://booklyfi.jasenkobozinovic.workers.dev"
GEMINI_DIRECT_BASE_URL = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com"


def get_gemini_url(model: str) -> str:
    # FIX: direktno na Google API, zaobiđi Cloudflare worker (timeout/404)
    return f"{GEMINI_DIRECT_BASE_URL}/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent"


def get_gemini_direct_url(model: str) -> str:
    return f"{GEMINI_DIRECT_BASE_URL}/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent"


def get_google_model_for_key(key_index: int) -> dict:
    """Return the pooled Google model assigned to a key index via modulo rotation."""
    return GOOGLE_MODEL_POOL[key_index % len(GOOGLE_MODEL_POOL)]


def get_next_google_model(current_model: str) -> dict:
    """Return the next Google model in the pool, or the first one if not found."""
    for i, model in enumerate(GOOGLE_MODEL_POOL):
        if model["model"] == current_model:
            return GOOGLE_MODEL_POOL[(i + 1) % len(GOOGLE_MODEL_POOL)]
    return GOOGLE_MODEL_POOL[0]


def get_model_api_string(profile_name: str, fallback: str = "") -> str:
    return AI_MODEL_STRINGS.get(profile_name, fallback)


@dataclass
class ProviderProfile:
    name: str  # uppercase ime ("GEMINI", "GROQ" ...)
    rpm_hard: int  # stvarni API limit
    rpm_safe: int  # naša granica (koristimo ovo)
    rpd_hard: int  # dnevni hard limit (0 = nepoznat/visok)
    rpd_safe: int  # naša dnevna granica
    tpm_hard: int  # token/min limit (0 = nebitan)
    min_gap_s: float  # min razmak između poziva JEDNOG ključa
    cooldown_429_s: float  # kratki cooldown na RPM 429 (ne kvota!)
    supports_system_role: bool  # prihvata {"role":"system"}?
    preferred_roles: List[str]  # uloge u kojima je odličan
    avoid_roles: List[str]  # uloge za koje je loš
    quality_tier: int  # 1=top, 2=solid, 3=ok, 4=fallback
    notes: str = ""

    @property
    def min_gap_for_key(self) -> float:
        """Minimalni razmak između poziva s jednog ključa (sigurni RPM limit)."""
        if self.rpm_safe <= 0:
            return 10.0
        return 60.0 / self.rpm_safe

    @property
    def daily_budget_per_key(self) -> int:
        """Sigurni dnevni budžet po ključu."""
        return self.rpd_safe

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# HELPER FUNKCIJE — koriste se iz api_fleet.py, rate_limiter.py i router-a
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def get_profile(provider: str) -> ProviderProfile | None:
    """Vraća profil za provajdera ili None ako nije poznat."""
    return PROVIDER_PROFILES.get(provider.upper())


def get_rpm_safe(provider: str) -> int:
    """Sigurni RPM limit za provajdera."""
    p = get_profile(provider)
    return p.rpm_safe if p else 15


def get_rpd_safe(provider: str) -> int:
    """Sigurni dnevni limit za provajdera (0 = neograničen)."""
    p = get_profile(provider)
    return p.rpd_safe if p else 1000


def get_min_gap(provider: str) -> float:
    """Minimalni razmak između poziva jednog ključa (sekunde)."""
    p = get_profile(provider)
    return p.min_gap_s if p else 5.0




# FIX #1: prva get_tpm_safe obrisana (dupla definicija)
def get_cooldown_429(provider: str) -> float:
    """Kratki cooldown na RPM 429 (ne dnevna kvota)."""
    p = get_profile(provider)
    return p.cooldown_429_s if p else 65.0


def is_preferred_for_role(provider: str, role: str) -> bool:
    p = get_profile(provider)
    return role.upper() in [r.upper() for r in (p.preferred_roles if p else [])]


def should_avoid_for_role(provider: str, role: str) -> bool:
    p = get_profile(provider)
    return role.upper() in [r.upper() for r in (p.avoid_roles if p else [])]


def get_quality_tier(provider: str) -> int:
    p = get_profile(provider)
    return p.quality_tier if p else 4


def effective_rpm_with_keys(provider: str, num_keys: int) -> int:
    """
    Ukupni efektivni RPM flote za provajdera s N ključeva.
    Korisno za logging i debug.
    """
    p = get_profile(provider)
    if not p:
        return 0
    return p.rpm_safe * num_keys


def effective_rpd_with_keys(provider: str, num_keys: int) -> int:
    """
    Ukupni efektivni dnevni kapacitet flote.
    """
    p = get_profile(provider)
    if not p:
        return 0
    if p.rpd_safe == 0:
        return 0  # neograničen
    return p.rpd_safe * num_keys


def print_fleet_capacity(fleet_keys: dict[str, list]) -> None:
    """
    Debug: ispiši kapacitet svake provajder flote.
    Primjer: print_fleet_capacity({"GEMINI": [...6 ključeva...], "GROQ": [...5...]})
    """
    print("\n=== FLEET KAPACITET ===")
    total_rpm = 0
    for prov, keys in sorted(fleet_keys.items()):
        n = len(keys)
        p = get_profile(prov)
        if not p:
            print(f"  {prov:<14} {n} ključ(a) — profil nije definiran")
            continue
        rpm_total = p.rpm_safe * n
        rpd_total = p.rpd_safe * n if p.rpd_safe else 0
        rpd_str = f"{rpd_total:,}/dan" if rpd_total else "neograničen/dan"
        total_rpm += rpm_total
        preferred = ", ".join(p.preferred_roles) or "sve"
        avoid = ", ".join(p.avoid_roles) or "—"
        print(
            f"  {prov:<14} {n} ključ(a) | "
            f"~{rpm_total:>3} RPM | {rpd_str:<18} | "
            f"tier={p.quality_tier} | OK za: {preferred:<30} | izbjegavaj: {avoid}"
        )
        if p.notes:
            print(f"  {'':14}   ⚠️  {p.notes}")
    print(f"\n  UKUPNO: ~{total_rpm} RPM efektivnih (svi ključevi)")
    print("=" * 60)

# TPM sigurnosne granice po provideru (tokens per minute).
# Vrijednosti su konzervativne — discovery može vratiti niže, ali ovo je
# donja granica koju rate_limiter NIKAD ne spušta ispod.
PROVIDER_TPM_SAFE = {
    "GEMINI":     250000,
    "GEMMA":      250000,
    "GROQ":       12000,      # FIX: bilo 6400 — output chunk ~7000 tok
    "CEREBRAS":   15000,
    "SAMBANOVA":  20000,
    "MISTRAL":    500000,
    "COHERE":     100000,
    "OPENROUTER": 20000,
    "GITHUB":     15000,
    "NVIDIA":     40000,      # NVIDIA NIM free tier ~40k TPM
    "CLOUDFLARE": 30000,      # Workers AI free tier
    "TOGETHER":   20000,
    "FIREWORKS":  20000,
    "CHUTES":     20000,
    "HUGGINGFACE":15000,
    "KLUSTER":    20000,
}


def get_tpm_safe(provider: str) -> float:
    """FIX #1: TPM sigurnosna granica (80% od tpm_hard iz profila).
    Vraća 0.0 ako profil nema TPM limit (gate isključen).
    Dinamički discovery iz response headera prepisuje ovo kad stigne."""
    p = get_profile(provider)
    if not p or getattr(p, "tpm_hard", 0) <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(p.tpm_hard * 0.8)

# ============================================================================
# API FLEET MANAGER V12.0 — api_fleet.py
#
# V12.0: Reintegrisan QuotaTracker — per-key i per-provider praćenje:
#   • RPM (klizni 60s prozor), RPD (dnevni), TPD (dnevni tokeni)
#   • Cooldown po ključu (RPM 429 → Retry-After ili profil default)
#   • Cooldown dnevne kvote (RPD iscrpljen → do slijedećeg reseta)
#   • Provider-level cooldown (IP ban ili globalni 429)
#   • Greške po HTTP kodu praćene i u KeyState i u QuotaTracker
#   • get_best_key() sada poštuje availability iz QuotaTracker-a
#
# FIX 19.05.2026:
#   • GOOGLE_MODEL_POOL ažuriran — mrtvi modeli uklonjeni, živi dodani
#   • analyze_response: parsira retryDelay iz Google JSON body-a
#   • Ako nema retryDelay ali je RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED — cooldown do ponoći UTC
# ============================================================================
