def _check_body_content(provider: str, body) -> tuple:
    """
    FIX: Provjeri je li 200 OK odgovor STVARNO koristan.
    Vraća (is_valid: bool, reason: str).
    
    GEMMA/GEMINI vraćaju 200 s:
      - prazan content (content=null, reasoning_content popunjen) — reasoning model
      - finishReason=PROHIBITED_CONTENT — safety filter
      - finishReason=MAX_TOKENS/LENGTH — odrezan odgovor
    """
    if not isinstance(body, dict):
        return True, ""  # ne možemo provjeriti, tretiraj kao OK
    
    prov_u = provider.upper()
    
    # Gemini/GEMMA native format
    if prov_u in ("GEMINI", "GEMMA"):
        candidates = body.get("candidates", [])
        if not candidates:
            return False, "nema candidates"
        cand = candidates[0]
        finish = (cand.get("finishReason") or "").upper()
        if finish == "PROHIBITED_CONTENT":
            return False, "PROHIBITED_CONTENT"
        if finish == "SAFETY":
            return False, "SAFETY"
        if finish == "RECITATION":
            return False, "RECITATION"
        # Provjeri ima li content.parts s tekstom
        content = cand.get("content", {})
        parts = content.get("parts", []) if isinstance(content, dict) else []
        has_text = any(isinstance(p, dict) and p.get("text") for p in parts)
        if not has_text:
            return False, f"prazan content (finish={finish or '?'})"
        if finish == "MAX_TOKENS":
            return False, "MAX_TOKENS (odrezano)"
        return True, ""
    
    # OpenAI-compat format (GROQ, NVIDIA, CLOUDFLARE, MISTRAL, ...)
    choices = body.get("choices", [])
    if not choices:
        return False, "nema choices"
    choice = choices[0]
    finish = (choice.get("finish_reason") or "").lower()
    if finish == "content_filter":
        return False, "content_filter"
    msg = choice.get("message", {})
    content = msg.get("content") if isinstance(msg, dict) else None
    
    # Reasoning modeli (muse-glimmer, nemotron): content=null, reasoning_content popunjen
    if not content or not str(content).strip():
        reasoning = msg.get("reasoning_content") if isinstance(msg, dict) else None
        if reasoning:
            return False, "reasoning-only (content=null)"
        return False, "prazan content"
    
    if finish == "length":
        return False, "length (odrezano)"
    
    return True, ""


class KeyState:
    """Stanje jednog API ključa — sadrži samo pozivne brojače."""

    def __init__(self, key: str, provider: str, saved: dict = None):
        self.key      = key
        self.provider = provider.upper()
        s = saved or {}

        self.total_requests: int   = 0  # FIX: pozivi se ne cuvaju izmedju sesija, uvijek 0 na startu
        self.last_used:      float = s.get("last_used", 0.0)

        self.calls_ok:       int  = s.get("calls_ok", 0)
        self._last_503_time: float = 0.0  # FIX sve_fix: zadnji 503 timestamp
        self.calls_failed:   int  = s.get("calls_failed", 0)
        self.calls_rejected: dict = {
            int(k): int(v)
            for k, v in s.get("calls_rejected", {}).items()
            if str(k).lstrip("-").isdigit()
        }

    @property
    def available(self) -> bool:
        ok, reason = quota_tracker.is_key_available(self.provider, self.key)
        if not ok:
            logger.debug(
                "[FleetManager] %s ...%s nedostupan: %s",
                self.provider, self.key[-4:], reason,
            )
        return ok

    def quota_info(self) -> dict:
        pq = quota_tracker._get_provider(self.provider)
        if not pq:
            return {}
        kq = pq.get_key(self.key)
        return kq.to_status_dict() if kq else {}

    @property
    def success_rate(self) -> float:
        total = self.calls_ok + self.calls_failed + sum(self.calls_rejected.values())
        if total == 0:
            return 1.0
        return round(self.calls_ok / total, 4)

    @property
    def masked(self) -> str:
        if len(self.key) <= 8:
            return "***"
        return self.key[:4] + "…" + self.key[-4:]

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "total_requests": self.total_requests,
            "last_used":      self.last_used,
            "calls_ok":       self.calls_ok,
            "calls_failed":   self.calls_failed,
            "calls_rejected": {str(k): v for k, v in self.calls_rejected.items()},
        }

    def to_ui_dict(self) -> dict:
        qi = self.quota_info()
        return {
            "key":            self.masked,
            "masked":         self.masked,
            "available":      self.available,
            "success_rate":   self.success_rate,
            "calls_ok":       self.calls_ok,
            "calls_failed":   self.calls_failed,
            "calls_rejected": {str(k): v for k, v in self.calls_rejected.items()},
            "total_requests": self.total_requests,
            "rpm":            qi.get("rpm_current", 0),
            "rpm_safe":       qi.get("rpm_safe", 0),
            "rpd":            qi.get("rpd_current", 0),
            "rpd_safe":       qi.get("rpd_safe", 0),
            "tpd":            qi.get("tpd_current", 0),
            "cooldown_s":     qi.get("cooldown_s", 0.0),
            "cooldown_reason": qi.get("cooldown_reason", ""),
        }


class FleetManager:
    """V12.0 Fleet Manager."""

    def __init__(self, config_path="dev_api.json", state_path="api_state.json"):
        self.config_path = Path(config_path)
        self.state_path  = Path(state_path)
        self.lock        = threading.Lock()
        self.fleet: dict = {}
        self.resolved_models: dict = {}
        self._rr_index: dict = {}
        self._last_call: dict = {}

        self._last_save_time: float = 0.0
        self._dirty: bool = False
        self._save_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._debounce_timer: threading.Timer | None = None

        self._load_config()
        self._resolve_models()

    def _load_config(self):
        try:
            raw = json.loads(self.config_path.read_text("utf-8"))
            logger.debug("[FleetManager] Konfiguracija učitana iz %s", self.config_path)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("[FleetManager] Nije moguće učitati konfiguraciju (%s): %s — flota prazna", self.config_path, e)
            raw = {}
        try:
            saved = json.loads(self.state_path.read_text("utf-8"))
            logger.debug("[FleetManager] Stanje ključeva učitano iz %s", self.state_path)
        except Exception:
            saved = {}

        # FIX: GEMMA mora UVIJEK dijeliti GEMINI ključeve (isti Google API
        # ključ radi za oba modela) — čak i kad korisnik kroz UI eksplicitno
        # dodaje posebne GEMMA-only ključeve. Stari kod je mirror radio SAMO
        # ako "GEMMA" nije postojao u configu — čim bi korisnik dodao ijedan
        # GEMMA ključ, mirror je stao i naslijeđeni GEMINI ključevi su tiho
        # ispali iz GEMMA rotacije. Sada se uvijek spaja i deduplicira:
        # GEMMA_efektivno = GEMINI ∪ GEMMA (GEMINI prvi u redoslijedu).
        if "GEMINI" in raw:
            def _as_key_list(v):
                if isinstance(v, list):
                    return [k for k in v if isinstance(k, str) and k.strip()]
                if isinstance(v, dict):
                    out = []
                    for kk, vv in v.items():
                        if isinstance(vv, dict) and "key" in vv:
                            out.append(vv["key"])
                        elif isinstance(vv, str):
                            out.append(vv)
                        else:
                            out.append(kk)
                    return [k for k in out if isinstance(k, str) and k.strip()]
                return []

            _gemini_list = _as_key_list(raw["GEMINI"])
            _gemma_list  = _as_key_list(raw.get("GEMMA"))
            _merged = list(_gemini_list)
            for _k in _gemma_list:
                if _k not in _merged:
                    _merged.append(_k)
            raw["GEMMA"] = _merged

        self.fleet = {}
        self._rr_index = {}

        for prov, data in raw.items():
            prov_u = prov.upper()
            if prov_u in {"EPUB_BACKGROUND", "PROXIES", "PROXIES_OFF"}:
                continue
            self.fleet[prov_u]    = []
            self._rr_index[prov_u] = 0

            key_list = []
            if isinstance(data, list):
                key_list = [k.strip() for k in data if isinstance(k, str) and k.strip()]
            elif isinstance(data, dict):
                for k, v in data.items():
                    if isinstance(v, dict) and "key" not in v:
                        key_list.append(k.strip())
                    elif isinstance(v, dict) and "key" in v:
                        key_list.append(v["key"].strip())
                    elif isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():
                        key_list.append(v.strip())

            prov_saved = saved.get(prov_u, {})
            for k_str in key_list:
                self.fleet[prov_u].append(
                    KeyState(k_str, prov_u, prov_saved.get(k_str))
                )
            logger.info("[FleetManager] %s: %d ključ(a) učitan(o)", prov_u, len(key_list))
            syslog.info("[FleetManager] %s: %d ključ(a) učitan(o)", prov_u, len(key_list))

        quota_tracker.initialize_from_fleet(self.fleet)
        syslog.info("[FleetManager] QuotaTracker inicijaliziran iz flote")

    def _resolve_models(self):
        from network.model_discovery import FALLBACK_MODELS
        self.resolved_models = dict(FALLBACK_MODELS)

    def _save_state(self):
        with self._save_lock:
            self._dirty = True
            now     = time.time()
            elapsed = now - self._last_save_time
            if elapsed >= _STATE_DEBOUNCE_INTERVAL:
                self._flush_state()
            else:
                if self._debounce_timer is None or not self._debounce_timer.is_alive():
                    remaining = _STATE_DEBOUNCE_INTERVAL - elapsed
                    self._debounce_timer = threading.Timer(remaining, self._flush_state_safe)
                    self._debounce_timer.daemon = True
                    self._debounce_timer.start()

    def _flush_state(self):
        if not self._dirty:
            return
        state = {
            p: {ks.key: ks.to_dict() for ks in keys}
            for p, keys in self.fleet.items()
        }
        try:
            self.state_path.write_text(
                json.dumps(state, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), "utf-8"
            )
            self._last_save_time = time.time()
            self._dirty = False
            logger.debug("[FleetManager] Stanje flushed u %s", self.state_path)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[FleetManager] Nije moguće snimiti stanje u %s: %s", self.state_path, e)

    def _flush_state_safe(self):
        with self._save_lock:
            self._flush_state()

    def flush_now(self):
        with self._save_lock:
            if self._debounce_timer:
                self._debounce_timer.cancel()
                self._debounce_timer = None
            self._flush_state()

    def reload(self):
        with self.lock:
            self._load_config()
            self._resolve_models()

    def get_active_model(self, provider_upper: str, static_fallback: str = "") -> str:
        from network.model_discovery import (
            get_cached_model,
            get_dead_models,
            get_cached_model_list,
            trigger_rediscover_background,
        )

        prov = provider_upper.upper()
        dead = get_dead_models(prov)

        discovered = get_cached_model(prov)
        if discovered and discovered not in dead:
            return discovered

        # Cached "best" model je mrtav (ili nema ga) -- probaj ostale
        # kandidate iz otkrivene liste prije pada na staticki fallback.
        for candidate in get_cached_model_list(prov):
            if candidate not in dead:
                return candidate

        fallback = self.resolved_models.get(prov, "") or static_fallback
        if fallback and fallback not in dead:
            return fallback

        # Sve sto znamo o ovom provajderu je mrtvo -- okini pozadinski
        # rediscover umjesto da tiho vracamo isti mrtvi ID zauvijek.
        try:
            key = self.get_best_key(prov)
            if key:
                trigger_rediscover_background(prov, key)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return fallback

    def get_best_key(self, provider: str):
        # FIX #6: uklonjen mrtav kod (503 filter koji se odmah preglašavao).
        # Filter 503-hladnih ključeva je integriran unutar lock bloka.
        prov_u = provider.upper()
        now = time.time()
        with self.lock:
            keys = self.fleet.get(prov_u, [])
            if not keys:
                # FIX 2026-09-26: DEBUG umjesto WARNING
                logger.debug("[FleetManager] get_best_key(%s): nema ključeva u floti", prov_u)
                return None

            # FIX #6: 503 filter unutar lock-a
            keys = [
                k for k in keys
                if (now - getattr(k, "_last_503_time", 0)) >= 60.0
            ]
            if not keys:
                logger.warning(
                    "[FleetManager] %s: svi ključevi 503-hladni (<60s)", prov_u
                )
                return None

            available = [ks for ks in keys if ks.available]
            if not available:
                reasons = []
                for ks in keys:
                    ok, reason = quota_tracker.is_key_available(prov_u, ks.key)
                    reasons.append(f"...{ks.key[-4:]}: {reason}")
                syslog.warning(
                    "[FleetManager] %s: svi ključevi nedostupni — %s",
                    prov_u, " | ".join(reasons),
                )
                logger.warning(
                    "[FleetManager] get_best_key(%s): svi ključevi nedostupni (%d ključ(a))",
                    prov_u, len(keys),
                )
                return None

            # RR rotacija s monotonim brojačem — ne ovisi o len(available).
            # Prethodni kod: index = rr % len(available) pa novi_rr = (index+1) % len(available)
            # Problem: kad available raste/pada (cooldown dolazi/odlazi) wraparound skoči
            # na isti ključ više puta uzastopno. Rješenje: rr je monotoni brojač,
            # biramo available[rr % len(available)] i inkrementujemo za 1.
            rr  = self._rr_index.get(prov_u, 0)
            idx = rr % len(available)
            chosen = available[idx]
            self._rr_index[prov_u] = rr + 1
            logger.debug(
                "[FleetManager] get_best_key(%s): odabran ...%s (success_rate=%.2f, %d/%d dostupno)",
                prov_u, chosen.key[-4:], chosen.success_rate, len(available), len(keys),
            )
            syslog.debug(
                "[FleetManager] get_best_key(%s): odabran ...%s (sr=%.2f, %d/%d dostupno)",
                prov_u, chosen.key[-4:], chosen.success_rate, len(available), len(keys),
            )
            return chosen.key

    def get_best_key_for_role(self, role: str):
        # FIX: preskoči providere bez ključeva BEZ log warningа (bilo je 40+ upozorenja
        # u 90 min za SAMBANOVA/MISTRAL koji uopće nemaju ključeve u dev_api.json).
        preferred = ROLE_PREFERRED_PROVIDERS.get(role.upper(), PROVIDER_ORDER)
        with self.lock:
            available_provs = {
                p.upper() for p, keys in self.fleet.items() if keys
            }
        # Filtriraj na providere koji imaju ključeve
        for prov in preferred:
            if prov.upper() not in available_provs:
                continue
            key = self.get_best_key(prov)
            if key:
                logger.debug("[FleetManager] get_best_key_for_role(%s): odabran %s ...%s",
                             role, prov, key[-4:])
                return prov, key
        for prov in PROVIDER_ORDER:
            if prov.upper() not in available_provs:
                continue
            if prov not in preferred:
                key = self.get_best_key(prov)
                if key:
                    logger.debug("[FleetManager] get_best_key_for_role(%s) fallback: %s ...%s",
                                 role, prov, key[-4:])
                    return prov, key
        logger.warning(
            "[FleetManager] get_best_key_for_role(%s): nema dostupnih ključeva",
            role,
        )
        return None, None

    def record_request(self, provider: str, key: str):
        """Zabilježi POKUŠAJ poziva (ne uspjeh)."""
        prov_u = provider.upper()
        for ks in self.fleet.get(prov_u, []):
            if ks.key == key:
                ks.total_requests += 1
                ks.last_used = time.time()
                return

    def record_success(self, provider: str, key: str):
        """Zabilježi USPJEŠAN poziv."""
        prov_u = provider.upper()
        for ks in self.fleet.get(prov_u, []):
            if ks.key == key:
                ks.calls_ok += 1
                return

    def record_failure(self, provider: str, key: str):
        """Zabilježi neuspješan poziv."""
        prov_u = provider.upper()
        for ks in self.fleet.get(prov_u, []):
            if ks.key == key:
                ks.total_requests += 1
                ks.calls_failed += 1
                return


    def record_usage(self, provider: str, key: str, req_count: int = 1, success: bool = True):
        prov_u = provider.upper()
        with self.lock:
            ks = self._find_key(prov_u, key)
            if not ks:
                return
            if success:
                ks.calls_ok += req_count
            else:
                ks.calls_failed += 1
        self._save_state()

    def record_network_failure(self, provider: str, key: str):
        prov_u = provider.upper()
        with self.lock:
            ks = self._find_key(prov_u, key)
            if ks:
                ks.calls_failed += 1
        self._save_state()

    def analyze_response(self, provider: str, key: str, status_code: int, headers, body=None):
        """
        Ažurira pozivne brojače ključa prema HTTP status kodu.
        Poziva QuotaTracker koji upravlja cooldown-om i kvotama.
        """
        prov_u = provider.upper()

        # Izvuci tokene iz body-a
        # OpenAI-compat format:  body["usage"]["total_tokens"] / prompt_tokens + completion_tokens
        # Gemini native format:  body["usageMetadata"]["totalTokenCount"]
        tokens = 0
        if isinstance(body, dict):
            usage = body.get("usage", {})
            if isinstance(usage, dict):
                tokens = int(usage.get("total_tokens", 0) or 0)
                if tokens == 0:
                    tokens = int(
                        (usage.get("prompt_tokens") or 0) +
                        (usage.get("completion_tokens") or 0)
                    )
            if tokens == 0:
                # Gemini native API
                meta = body.get("usageMetadata", {})
                if isinstance(meta, dict):
                    tokens = int(meta.get("totalTokenCount", 0) or 0)

        # Izvuci Retry-After iz headera
        retry_after = None
        if headers:
            for hdr in ("Retry-After", "retry-after", "x-ratelimit-reset-requests"):
                val = headers.get(hdr)
                if val:
                    try:
                        retry_after = float(val)
                    except (TypeError, ValueError):
                        pass
                    break

        # FIX: Google Gemini ne šalje Retry-After header za RPD — šalje retryDelay u JSON body-u
        # Format: {"error": {"details": [{"retryDelay": "86400s"}]}}
        if retry_after is None and status_code == 429 and isinstance(body, dict):
            try:
                details = body.get("error", {}).get("details", [])
                for d in details:
                    rd = d.get("retryDelay", "")
                    if rd and isinstance(rd, str) and rd.endswith("s"):
                        retry_after = float(rd[:-1])
                        logger.info(
                            "[FleetManager] %s ...%s retryDelay iz body-a: %.0fs",
                            prov_u, key[-4:], retry_after,
                        )
                        break
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Ako je RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED bez retryDelay — cooldown do ponoći UTC + 1 min buffer
            if retry_after is None:
                import datetime
                err_msg    = str(body.get("error", {}).get("message", "")).lower()
                err_status = str(body.get("error", {}).get("status",  "")).lower()
                if "resource_exhausted" in err_status or "quota" in err_msg:
                    utc_now  = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
                    midnight = (utc_now + datetime.timedelta(days=1)).replace(
                        hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0
                    )
                    retry_after = (midnight - utc_now).total_seconds() + 60.0
                    logger.info(
                        "[FleetManager] %s ...%s RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED bez retryDelay "
                        "— cooldown do ponoći UTC: %.0fs (%.1fh)",
                        prov_u, key[-4:], retry_after, retry_after / 3600,
                    )
                    syslog.warning(
                        "[FleetManager] %s ...%s GEMINI RPD iscrpljen "
                        "— automatski cooldown %.1fh do ponoći UTC",
                        prov_u, key[-4:], retry_after / 3600,
                    )

                # FIX 21.05.2026: RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED (RPM 429) — nema retryDelay ni header.
                # Gemini šalje ovaj status za RPM prekoračenje. Bez cooldowna → burst → 401 ban.
                # Koristimo cooldown_429_s iz profila (12s za Gemini).
                elif "rate_limit_exceeded" in err_status or "rate limit" in err_msg:
                    try:
                        from config.ai_config import get_cooldown_429
                        retry_after = get_cooldown_429(prov_u)
                    except Exception:
                        retry_after = 15.0
                    logger.info(
                        "[FleetManager] %s ...%s RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED bez retryDelay "
                        "— cooldown iz profila: %.0fs",
                        prov_u, key[-4:], retry_after,
                    )
                    syslog.warning(
                        "[FleetManager] %s ...%s RPM 429 RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED "
                        "— cooldown %.0fs",
                        prov_u, key[-4:], retry_after,
                    )

        # QuotaTracker — cooldown, kvote, tokeni
        quota_tracker.record_response(
            provider=prov_u,
            key=key,
            status_code=status_code,
            tokens=tokens,
            retry_after=retry_after,
            headers=headers,
        )

        with self.lock:
            ks = self._find_key(prov_u, key)
            if not ks:
                return

            if status_code == 200:
                # FIX: provjeri je li odgovor STVARNO koristan.
                # Reasoning modeli (muse-glimmer) vraćaju content=null.
                # Gemini vraća finishReason=PROHIBITED_CONTENT.
                _valid, _reason = _check_body_content(prov_u, body)
                if not _valid:
                    ks.calls_failed += 1
                    ks.last_used = time.time()
                    logger.warning(
                        "[FleetManager] %s ...%s → 200 ali nevalidan: %s (failed=%d)",
                        prov_u, key[-4:], _reason, ks.calls_failed,
                    )
                    syslog.warning(
                        "[FleetManager] %s ...%s → 200 ali nevalidan: %s",
                        prov_u, key[-4:], _reason,
                    )
                    try:
                        from network.quota_tracker import quota_tracker
                        # FIX #8: ispravni pozivi — set_key_cooldown postoji,
                        # record_error na QuotaTracker ne postoji.
                        quota_tracker.set_key_cooldown(prov_u, key, 90.0, _reason)
                        logger.debug(
                            "[FleetManager] %s ...%s nevalidan 200 — cooldown 90s (%s)",
                            prov_u, key[-4:], _reason,
                        )
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.debug(
                            "[FleetManager] neuspjelo postavljanje cooldowna: %s", e
                        )
                else:
                    ks.calls_ok += 1
                    logger.debug(
                        "[FleetManager] %s ...%s → 200 OK (ok=%d, tokeni=%d)",
                        prov_u, key[-4:], ks.calls_ok, tokens,
                    )
                    syslog.debug(
                        "[FleetManager] %s ...%s → 200 OK (ok=%d, tokeni=%d)",
                        prov_u, key[-4:], ks.calls_ok, tokens,
                    )
            elif status_code >= 500:
                ks.calls_failed += 1
                logger.warning(
                    "[FleetManager] %s ...%s → %d server error (failed=%d)",
                    prov_u, key[-4:], status_code, ks.calls_failed,
                )
                syslog.warning(
                    "[FleetManager] %s ...%s → %d server error (failed=%d)",
                    prov_u, key[-4:], status_code, ks.calls_failed,
                )
            elif status_code >= 400:
                ks.calls_rejected[status_code] = ks.calls_rejected.get(status_code, 0) + 1
                logger.warning(
                    "[FleetManager] %s ...%s → %d odbijen (rejected[%d]=%d)",
                    prov_u, key[-4:], status_code, status_code,
                    ks.calls_rejected[status_code],
                )
                syslog.warning(
                    "[FleetManager] %s ...%s → %d odbijen (rejected[%d]=%d, retry_after=%s)",
                    prov_u, key[-4:], status_code, status_code,
                    ks.calls_rejected[status_code],
                    f"{retry_after:.0fs}" if retry_after else "n/a",
                )

        self._save_state()

    def get_fleet_summary(self) -> dict:
        with self.lock:
            summary = {}
            for prov, keys in self.fleet.items():
                summary[prov] = {
                    "total":        len(keys),
                    "calls_ok":     sum(k.calls_ok for k in keys),
                    "calls_failed": sum(k.calls_failed for k in keys),
                }
        return summary

    @staticmethod
    def _prov_ui_entry(keys: list) -> dict:
        ui_keys = [ks.to_ui_dict() for ks in keys]
        sr = round(sum(k["success_rate"] for k in ui_keys) / len(ui_keys), 4) if ui_keys else 1.0
        return {"total": len(keys), "success_rate": sr, "keys": ui_keys}

    def get_fleet_ui(self) -> dict:
        with self.lock:
            result = {}
            for prov in PROVIDER_ORDER:
                keys = self.fleet.get(prov, [])
                if not keys:
                    continue
                result[prov] = self._prov_ui_entry(keys)
            for prov, keys in self.fleet.items():
                if prov not in result and keys:
                    result[prov] = self._prov_ui_entry(keys)
        return result

    def get_total_active_keys(self) -> int:
        # PROBLEM #2 fix: broji samo dostupne ključeve (available=True),
        # ne sve — ključevi u cooldownu ne smiju utjecati na window_size
        with self.lock:
            return sum(
                1 for keys in self.fleet.values()
                for ks in keys if ks.available
            )

    def _find_key(self, prov_u: str, key_str: str):
        for ks in self.fleet.get(prov_u, []):
            if ks.key == key_str:
                return ks
        return None
